### [148] [The Position Space Chern Number: A Topological Index for Chiral Magnetic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28549)
*Zachariah Addison*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分类绝缘手性磁性系统拓扑结构的新指标——位置空间陈数（C_R），它区别于动量空间陈数（C_K），并证明其非零值保障了动量空间中局域势垒边界上拓扑保护隙内态的存在，同时揭示了C_R与经典斯格明子绕数的联系，以及C_R和C_K不可同时非零的排斥现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的拓扑分类主要基于动量空间陈数（C_K），但对于某些手性磁性系统，动量空间的拓扑描述不够充分，需要引入位置空间的拓扑指标来完整刻画其物理性质。

Method: 定义位置空间陈数C_R作为拓扑分类指标；利用Chern-Simons有效场论描述位置空间陈绝缘体；通过动量空间中横向力算子的拓扑量子化关联进行数学刻画；在斯格明子磁相系统中验证C_R的非零存在性。

Result: C_R非零值保障动量空间中局域势垒边界上拓扑保护隙内态的存在；Chern-Simons场论揭示动量空间中量子流与横向力算子的拓扑量子化关联；C_R推广了经典斯格明子绕数概念；发现C_R与C_K存在竞争排斥，不能同时非零。

Conclusion: 位置空间陈数C_R是手性磁性系统拓扑分类的有效新指标，与动量空间陈数C_K形成互补的拓扑描述框架，两者不可同时非零的约束揭示了位置空间与动量空间拓扑之间的深层竞争关系。

Abstract: This paper introduces an index that categorizes the topology of insulating chiral magnetic systems. The position space Chern number, $C_R$ is distinct from its momentum space counterpart, $C_K$. A nonzero index guarantees the existence of topologically protected in-gap states that localize on the edge of local potential barriers in momentum space. The Chern-Simons effective field theory describing position space Chern insulators reveals a topologically quantized correlation between transverse force operators that describe the flow of quanta in momentum space. We demonstrate the existence of nonzero $C_R$ in systems hosting skyrmion magnetic phases and show how the index generalizes the classical concept of a skyrmion winding number. Lastly we investigate the competition between momentum space and position space topologies, and highlight an apparent obstruction to having systems with both $C_R \neq 0$ and $C_K \neq 0$.

</details>


### [149] [TetMaG-Guided Design and Operando Electron Holography Validation of Current-Induced Domain-Wall Motion in 3D Curved and Cornered Fe Nanobridges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28576)
*Sameh Okasha,Shriyar Tariq,Attila Kákay,Ryan Yang,Gregor Hlawacek,Akhil G. Nair,Rafal Dunin-Borkowski*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文结合TetMaG微磁模拟、FEBID制备和离轴电子全息表征，研究了三维磁性纳米桥中曲率和拐角几何结构对磁畴壁行为的影响。拐角结构表现出优先畴壁钉扎效应，而弯曲结构促进磁化逐渐旋转。通过电子全息术实现了对模拟结果的定量实验验证，并展示了电流驱动畴壁运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维磁性纳米结构为突破平面系统的限制、控制磁畴壁构型提供了新机遇，但实现可靠的三维磁性器件需要精确控制几何依赖的畴壁行为并进行定量实验验证，当前缺乏对曲率和拐角几何影响的系统研究。

Method: 使用TetMaG微磁模拟预测不同几何结构的磁性能；采用聚焦电子束诱导沉积（FEBID）制备高结构保真度的三维磁性纳米桥；利用离轴电子全息术对制备结构进行定量磁感应映射表征，并与模拟结果对比验证。

Result: 拐角纳米桥表现为优先畴壁钉扎位点，稳定局域磁构型并实现相邻钉扎位置间的可控切换；弯曲纳米桥促进磁化逐渐旋转、降低钉扎并实现更平滑的畴壁运动。电子全息测量获得的磁感应图与模拟结果匹配，提供了直接实验验证。Operando电流实验观察到拐角结构中尾对尾畴壁向头对尾构型的位移。

Conclusion: 几何结构在调控三维磁性系统行为中起关键作用，曲率和拐角几何导致截然不同的畴壁特性，为设计基于三维磁性纳米结构的新型器件提供了实验依据和设计准则。

Abstract: Three-dimensional (3D) magnetic nanostructures offer new opportunities for controlling domain-wall (DW) configurations beyond the limitations of planar systems, providing promising architectures. However, the realization of reliable 3D magnetic devices requires precise control of geometry-dependent DW behaviour and quantitative experimental validation of the resulting magnetic states. Here, we combine TetMaG micromagnetic simulations, focused electron beam induced deposition (FEBID), and off-axis electron holography to investigate the influence of curvature and corner geometries on DW behaviour in 3D magnetic nanobridges. TetMaG simulations predict fundamentally different magnetic properties for curved and cornered geometries. Cornered nanobridges act as preferential DW pinning sites, stabilizing localized magnetic configurations and enabling controlled switching between neighbouring pinning positions. While curved nanobridges promote gradual magnetization rotation, reduced pinning, and smoother DW motion. These optimized geometries were fabricated with high structural fidelity using FEBID and subsequently characterized by quantitative electron holography. Electron holography measurements revealed magnetic induction maps that matched the simulated magnetization configurations, providing direct experimental validation of the TetMaG predictions. Curved and cornered geometries exhibited distinct DW characteristics governed by their local structural features, demonstrating the critical role of geometry in tailoring magnetic behaviour in 3D systems. Operando current-biasing experiments further revealed current-induced DW motion, including the displacement of a tail-to-tail DW into a head-to-tail configuration within corner structures.

</details>


### [150] [Voltage-tunable Josephson Junctions on Germanium Quantum Wells with in-situ Aluminum Contacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28585)
*Joshua P. Thompson,Jason T. Dong,Bernardo Langa,Chomani K. Gaspe,Riss Card,Brycelynn Bailey,Shiva Davari,Bethany E. Matthews,Matthew J. Olszta,Silas Hoffman,Thomas M. Hazard,Kyle Serniak,Hugh O. H. Churchill,Kasra Sardashti,Christopher J. K. Richardson*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种深台面刻蚀工艺，将Ge量子阱VT-JJ集成到低损耗浮区硅衬底上，实现了超过100 nA的栅极可调超电流和8.63 μV的I_cR_n乘积，为VT-JJ与量子电路元件的集成提供了可行路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VT-JJ在超导量子电子学中具有重要潜力，但由于未解决的半导体微波损耗问题，其与量子电路的集成仍面临挑战。

Method: 采用深台面刻蚀工艺去除台面器件以外的外延材料，将VT-JJ置于低损耗浮区硅衬底上；通过MBE在浮区硅衬底上生长Ge量子阱并原位沉积铝接触，形成无氧化物的超导体-半导体界面；优化深台面刻蚀工艺以获得足够侧壁倾斜度，实现从衬底到台面顶部的连续金属沉积。

Result: 制备的约瑟夫森结展示了栅极可调超电流，最大临界电流超过100 nA，临界电流-正常电阻乘积为8.63 μV。

Conclusion: 该结果为改善电压可调超导电路元件与量子电子构建模块（如耦合器和量子比特）的集成提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Voltage-tunable Josephson junctions (VT-JJs) are an emerging element in superconducting quantum electronics with potential to expand the functionality of conventional designs. While VT-JJs are largely compatible with wafer-scale semiconductor processing, their integration into quantum circuits remains a challenge due to unmitigated semiconductor microwave loss. Here, a deep mesa etch process, wherein the epitaxial material is removed except the VT-JJ device, will facilitate the integration of VT-JJs with low-microwave-loss circuit elements by allowing these circuit elements to be placed directly on a low-loss substrate. A Germanium quantum well is grown by Molecular Beam Epitaxy (MBE) on a float zone silicon substrate with in-situ deposited aluminum contacts. This combination allows the formation of an oxide-free superconductor-semiconductor interface. The deep mesa etch process is optimized to produce a sidewall taper sufficient for continuous metal deposition from the substrate to the top of the mesa for electrostatic gate electrodes and interconnects. The fabricated Josephson junctions demonstrate gate-tunable supercurrents with a maximum critical current over 100 nA and critical-current normal-resistance product of $8.63~μV$. These results demonstrate a pathway toward improved integration of voltage-tunable superconducting circuit elements with quantum electronic building blocks such as couplers and qubits.

</details>


### [151] [Graphene as a Tunable Nonradiative Bath for Moiré Excitons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28591)
*Katsunori Wakabayashi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个最小化理论模型，描述二维激子（尤其是莫尔局域激子）向邻近石墨烯层的非辐射能量转移。通过费米黄金定则，将转移速率表示为激子近场光谱与石墨烯长波电子损失函数的重叠，并考虑激子形态因子。研究发现，当激子局域长度大于激子-石墨烯间距时，高动量近场成分被滤除，从而可通过光致发光（PL）猝灭探测激子局域化。石墨烯作为门可调谐的电子库，其Pauli阻塞效应可抑制带间电子-空穴激发，部分恢复PL强度和寿命。将结果映射到实验相关参数范围，证明石墨烯栅极不仅是无源静电元件，更是可调谐的二维电子储层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立描述二维激子（特别是莫尔超晶格中局域激子）向石墨烯非辐射能量转移的最小化理论模型，从而理解过渡金属硫化物/六方氮化硼/石墨烯异质结构中的PL猝灭机制，并探索利用PL猝灭作为探测激子局域化和石墨烯电子结构的实验探针。

Method: 基于费米黄金定则，将能量转移速率表达为激子近场光谱与石墨烯长波电子损失函数的重叠积分，并引入高斯形式因子描述莫尔激子的有限空间展宽。采用Pauli阻塞模型描述石墨烯中带间激发的抑制效应，并与完整随机相位近似（RPA）损失函数进行对比验证。

Result: 点偶极极限下重现了经典的∝z⁻⁴能量转移律；当激子局域长度≳间距时，高动量近场成分被滤除，转移速率降低；Pauli阻塞模型预测当2|μ_F|接近ℏω时，带间激发被强烈抑制，PL部分恢复；最小模型与完整RPA结果在相关距离范围内误差在几个百分点以内。

Conclusion: 石墨烯栅极不仅是无源静电元件，而且是可调谐的二维电子储层，其长波响应可通过激子PL猝灭进行探测。该模型为理解TMD/hBN/石墨烯异质结构的光学响应提供了理论框架。

Abstract: A minimal theory for the nonradiative transfer of energy from a two-dimensional (2D) exciton -- especially a moiré-localized exciton -- to a nearby graphene layer is presented. Starting from Fermi's golden rule, the transfer rate is written as the overlap between the exciton near-field spectrum and the long-wavelength electronic loss function of graphene, weighted by an exciton form factor. In the point-dipole limit the framework reproduces the established $\GET\propto z^{-4}$ law for energy transfer to graphene. Including the finite spatial extent of a moiré exciton through a Gaussian form factor with localization length $\lX$, we show that high-momentum components of the near field are filtered out for $z\lesssim\lX$, so that the transfer rate -- and hence the photoluminescence (PL) quenching -- can serve as a probe of exciton localization. Treating graphene as a gate-tunable bath, a Pauli-blocking model predicts that interband electron-hole excitations are strongly suppressed once $2|\muF|$ approaches $\hbarω$, partially restoring PL intensity and lifetime. Benchmarking against the full random-phase-approximation loss function of doped graphene confirms the minimal model to within a few percent over the relevant distance range for representative near-infrared exciton parameters. We map the resulting PL observables over experimentally relevant ranges of spacer thickness, localization length, emission energy, and Fermi level, and identify when graphene-induced quenching dominates the optical response of transition-metal dichalcogenide/hexagonal boron nitride/graphene heterostructures. A graphene gate thus acts not as a passive electrostatic element but as a tunable 2D electronic reservoir whose long-wavelength response can be probed through exciton PL quenching.

</details>


### [152] [Direct observation of interfacial exchange coupling in a magnetic tunnel junction through spin-polarized quasiparticle interference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28686)
*Xu Wang,Chenxi Wang,Ying Yang,Yining Hu,Qingle Zhang,Chen Chen,Donglai Feng,Tong Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文利用自旋极化扫描隧道显微镜（SP-STM）和准粒子干涉技术，直接观测了Fe包覆针尖与Cr(001)表面形成的磁性隧道结中的界面交换耦合，发现铁磁针尖引起Cr(001)自旋极化表面态高达10 meV的能量偏移，且该偏移对针尖-表面距离和自旋对齐高度敏感，证明了扩展的二维表面态可以跨异质结介导强交换耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 界面交换耦合是磁性异质结中实现自旋三重态超导、量子反常霍尔效应等新奇现象的关键，但其微观表征在技术上极具挑战性，因此迫切需要一种能够直接观测和表征界面交换耦合的方法。

Method: 采用自旋极化扫描隧道显微镜（SP-STM）结合准粒子干涉技术，以Fe包覆针尖作为铁磁探针，在Cr(001)表面构建磁性隧道结，通过测量自旋极化表面态的能量偏移来直接观测界面交换耦合。

Result: 发现Fe铁磁针尖诱导Cr(001)自旋极化表面态产生高达10 meV的显著能量偏移，该偏移强烈依赖于针尖-表面距离以及Fe针尖与Cr表面的自旋对齐方向，且可通过外磁场切换自旋对齐状态。

Conclusion: 扩展的二维表面态能够跨异质结介导强交换耦合，为局部调控界面交换相互作用诱导的物理现象提供了新的途径。

Abstract: Interfacial exchange coupling plays a critical role in enabling novel phenomena in magnetic heterostructures, such as spin triplet superconductivity, quantum anomalous Hall effect (QAHE), and advanced spintronic functionalities. While microscopic characterization of this coupling is essential for elucidating the underlying mechanism, it remains technically challenging. Here, using spin-polarized scanning tunneling microscopy (SP-STM) and quasiparticle interference, we directly observed interfacial exchange coupling in a magnetic tunnel junction formed by an Fe coated tip and a Cr(001) surface. We found the ferromagnetic tip induces significant energy shift (up to 10 meV) in the spin-polarized surface state of Cr(001). This shift is highly sensitive to the tip-surface distance and the spin-alignment between Fe tip and Cr surface, which can be switched by external magnetic field. Our results demonstrate that extended 2D surface states can mediate strong exchange coupling across a heterojunction, enabling local control of interfacial exchange interaction induced phenomena.

</details>


### [153] [Multi-level π-junction in a proximitized Ge/SiGe quantum dot probed by an on-chip superconducting microwave resonator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28762)
*Luigi Ruggiero,Vera Jo Weibel,Pauline Drexler,Carlo Ciaccia,Christian Olsen,Dominique Bougeard,Christian Schönenberger,Andrea Hofmann*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过片上微波测量技术，研究了锗/硅锗异质结中由邻近化量子点形成的多能级π结。在多个量子点轨道同时参与超导输运的多能级体系中，约瑟夫森基态不再仅由单个轨道占据决定。结合直流输运与微波技术，作者识别了多能级π结在栅压依赖性和微波响应中的定性特征，尤其观察到栅压调控下尖锐或平滑的相变及其不同的电感和耗散特征。该工作将锗基邻近化体系确立为研究混合超导/半导体物理的平台，并展示了栅极定义超导量子器件与高品质片上微波谐振器的集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多能级π结此前仅在碳纳米管等极干净体系中观察到，本文旨在将这类研究拓展到锗/硅锗异质结体系，验证其作为研究混合超导/半导体物理的可行平台，并展示片上微波谐振器与栅极定义超导量子器件的集成能力。

Method: 采用片上微波测量结合直流输运技术，对锗/硅锗异质结中邻近化量子点形成的多能级π结进行表征，分析其栅压依赖性和微波响应中的电感和耗散特征。

Result: 观察到多能级π结在栅压调控下存在尖锐或平滑的相变，并展现出不同的电感和耗散特征，这是多能级约瑟夫森输运的定性标志。

Conclusion: 邻近化锗基体系可作为研究混合超导/半导体物理的有效平台，且实现了高品质片上微波谐振器与栅极定义超导量子器件的集成。

Abstract: Using on-chip microwave measurements, we investigate multilevel $π$-junctions formed by proximitized quantum dot (QD) in a germanium (Ge)/silicon-germanium (SiGe) heterostructure. In the multilevel regime, where several QD orbitals contribute simultaneously to superconducting transport, the Josephson ground state is no longer determined solely by the occupation of a single orbital. By combining DC transport and microwave techniques, we identify the qualitative signatures of multilevel $π$-junctions in both their gate-voltage dependence and microwave response. In particular, we observe combinations phase transitions that are sharp or smooth in gate voltage and which exhibit distinct inductive and dissipative signatures. Such multilevel Josephson transport has previously been observed primarily in exceptionally clean systems such as carbon nanotubes. Our results establish proximitized Ge as a platform for investigating hybrid superconductor/semiconductor physics and demonstrate the integration of gate-defined superconducting quantum devices with high-quality on-chip microwave resonators.

</details>


### [154] [Inter-band coherence effects in disordered crystals: beyond the non-crossing approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28809)
*Zhanning Wang,James H. Cullen,Roberto Raimondi,Dimitrie Culcer*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文发展了超越非交叉近似的Bloch电子量子动力学理论，在长度规范和Gaussian白噪声无序下将杂质散射纳入第四阶，推导出包含自能修正、梯型顶点重正化和交叉量子干涉贡献的碰撞积分，并应用于二维大质量Dirac费米子模型，发现交叉杂质过程产生与内禀Berry曲率项共存的τ^0阶外在贡献，其中Ψ型项抵消而X型项保持有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的非平衡密度矩阵形式在非交叉近似下无法捕获名义上为无序零阶的稳态项，这类项与内禀能带几何响应（如反常霍尔效应及相关的自旋、轨道、谷输运）相互竞争，因此需要将理论框架扩展到非交叉近似之外。

Method: 在长度规范和Gaussian白噪声无序下，将杂质散射展开到无序势的第四阶；通过对杂质诱导的密度矩阵涨落进行迭代求解，在减去非连通杂质配对后得到连通的V^4碰撞积分，从而避免双重计数。所得项分为自能修正、梯型顶点重正化和交叉量子干涉贡献，并澄清了该密度矩阵动力学方程与Keldysh形式论的对应关系，将响应分解为Fermi面与Fermi海两部分。

Result: 在二维大质量Dirac费米子模型中，解析得到了Born级单粒子寿命、输运弛豫时间和纵向电导率；进一步计算了包含交叉杂质过程的反常霍尔电导率，发现交叉过程产生与内禀Berry曲率项共存的τ^0阶外在贡献；对于Gaussian白噪声无序，Ψ型贡献抵消而X型项保持有限。

Conclusion: 该形式论为将能带几何与交叉无序修正纳入多能带输运提供了自洽的路径，可推广应用于自旋、赝自旋、轨道和谷现象的研究。

Abstract: We develop a quantum kinetic theory for Bloch electrons driven by a uniform dc electric field, extending the nonequilibrium density-matrix formalism beyond the non-crossing approximation. This extension is required to capture steady-state terms that are nominally zeroth order in disorder strength and compete with intrinsic band-geometric responses, as in anomalous Hall and related spin, orbital, and valley transport. Working in the length gauge with Gaussian white-noise disorder, we include impurity scattering to fourth order in the disorder potential. An iterative solution for the impurity-induced density-matrix fluctuations yields a connected $V^4$ collision integral after subtracting disconnected impurity pairings, thereby avoiding double counting. The resulting terms separate into self-energy corrections, ladder-type vertex renormalization, and crossed quantum-interference contributions. We clarify the correspondence between this density-matrix kinetic equation and the Keldysh formalism, and decompose the response into Fermi-surface and Fermi-sea components. As an application, we study the two-dimensional massive Dirac fermion model. We obtain analytical expressions for the single-particle lifetime, transport relaxation time, and longitudinal conductivity at the Born level, and then evaluate the anomalous Hall conductivity including crossed impurity processes. These processes generate an extrinsic contribution of order $τ^0$ that coexists with the intrinsic Berry-curvature term; for Gaussian white-noise disorder in this model, the $Ψ$-type contribution cancels while the $X$-type term remains finite. The formalism provides a consistent route for incorporating band geometry and crossed-disorder corrections into multiband transport, with applications to spin, pseudospin, orbital, and valley phenomena.

</details>


### [155] [Hall viscosity from metric-sensitive dichroic probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30051)
*Alberto Nardin,Bruno Mera,Anaïs Defossez,Baptiste Bermond,Tomoki Ozawa,Nathan Goldman*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于圆二色性的光谱探针，通过手性度量敏感驱动（旋转四极“鞍”形扰动）直接测量量子霍尔液滴的霍尔粘度，并实现了频率分辨和空间分辨的测量方案，适用于冷原子等量子工程平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 霍尔粘度作为量子霍尔液滴对度规变形的几何响应，长期以来难以直接测量，现有方法存在局限性。

Method: 利用圆二色性光谱技术，施加旋转四极（鞍形）扰动作为手性度量敏感驱动，模拟度规调制并耦合面积保持变形生成元，通过二向色信号提取霍尔粘度，并结合频率分辨光谱分离其他激发，进一步提出局域化公式以实现空间分辨标记。

Result: 圆二色性信号可直接测量霍尔粘度；频率分辨光谱可将其与其他激发解耦；局域化方案适用于连续和晶格体系。

Conclusion: 该工作为在量子工程平台（如光晶格冷原子）中直接测量霍尔粘度开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Hall viscosity characterizes the geometric response of a quantum Hall droplet to deformations of the underlying metric, yet it has remained difficult to measure directly. We propose a spectroscopic probe based on circular dichroism, using chiral metric-sensitive drives -- implemented as rotating quadrupolar ("saddle") perturbations -- that effectively modulate the metric and couple to the generators of area-preserving deformations. The resulting dichroic signal directly measures the Hall viscosity, while frequency-resolved spectroscopy disentangles it from other excitations. A local formulation further enables spatially resolved markers of Hall viscosity applicable to both continuum and lattice systems. Our results open a direct route to measuring Hall viscosity in quantum-engineered platforms such as cold atoms in optical lattices.

</details>


### [156] [Topological phase and its effective tuning in a ladder lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28816)
*Qi-Bo Zeng*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个由SSH链和普通1D紧束缚链组成的二腿梯子模型，发现即使SSH腿处于平庸区域，通过调节腿间耦合也能产生具有零能边缘态的拓扑非平庸相。拓扑非平庸区域相比单一SSH模型显著扩大，并通过量子化贝里相进行表征。非平庸区域可进一步分为两个子区域，分别对应边缘态位于不同腿上的情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索通过平庸晶格与拓扑晶格之间的耦合来调控和诱导拓扑相与边缘态的可能性，拓展拓扑物理的研究平台。

Method: 构建SSH链与普通紧束缚链组成的二腿梯子模型；通过调节腿间耦合强度分析拓扑相变；利用量子化贝里相表征拓扑相；解析确定相边界；分析零能边缘模在梯子中的分布。

Result: 即使SSH腿处于拓扑平庸区域，腿间耦合也能诱导出具有零能边缘态的拓扑非平庸相；拓扑非平庸区域较单一SSH模型显著扩大；非平庸区域可进一步分为两个子区域，分别对应边缘态位于不同腿上的情况。

Conclusion: 梯子晶格中可通过平庸晶格的调控有效调节拓扑相和边缘模，为研究拓扑相提供了新平台。

Abstract: We study a two-leg ladder model consists of a one-dimensional (1D) Su-Schrieffer-Heeger (SSH) lattice with staggered nearest-neighboring hopping amplitudes and a normal 1D tight-binding lattice with uniform hopping. By varying the strength of inter-leg coupling, we find that topologically nontrivial phase with zero-energy edge modes will emerge, even when the SSH leg is in the trivial regime. Compared with the single SSH model, the nontrivial region in the parameter space is significantly expanded in the ladder. The topological phase is characterized by quantized Berry phase, and the phase boundaries are determined analytically. We also analyze the distributions of topological zero modes in the ladder, and find that the nontrivial regime can be further divided into two regions, which are separated by a gap closing point in the energy spectrum and correspond to the cases with edge modes residing in different legs. These results indicate that the topological phase and edge modes can be effectively tuned through the manipulations in the trivial lattice. Our work unveils the emergence of nontrivial topology in the ladder lattices and provides a new platform for studying topological phases.

</details>


### [157] [Perfect elliptic dichroism: Probing the metric of anisotropic quantum Hall droplets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30052)
*Bruno Mera,Alberto Nardin,Anaïs Defossez,Baptiste Bermond,Tomoki Ozawa,Nathan Goldman*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出通过设计二色性探针的几何结构并识别"完美椭圆二色性"的阈值，来探测量子霍尔液滴的内在几何结构（固有度规）。该方法可自然推广至理想陈带，并在Harper-Hofstadter模型等晶格体系中揭示了有限晶格间距修正导致的朗道轨道度规重整化效应，为实验观测提供了光谱学窗口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解量子霍尔体系的几何结构是现代凝聚态物理的核心挑战之一，现有方法难以直接探测量子霍尔液滴的内在度规这一几何量。

Method: 通过工程化设计椭圆偏振二色性探针，识别体系仅对特定手性椭圆偏振驱动响应的"完美椭圆二色性"阈值，将其与液滴内在度规直接关联。

Result: 在理想陈带中，占据态的全纯性保证了单一手性吸收率的消失和另一手性的量化响应；在Harper-Hofstadter模型等晶格实现中，有限晶格间距修正导致朗道轨道度规的重整化，完美二色性对应的探针椭圆率发生偏移。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了量子霍尔相的丰富几何结构，并为在量子工程化平台上观测这些效应提供了具体实验路径。

Abstract: Understanding the geometry of quantum Hall systems is a central challenge in modern condensed matter physics. We introduce a framework for probing the geometric structure of quantum Hall droplets by engineering the geometry of a dichroic probe and identifying the onset of "perfect elliptic dichroism", a regime in which the system responds exclusively to an elliptically polarized drive of a given chirality. This phenomenon provides a direct diagnostic of the droplet's intrinsic metric, and we show that it extends naturally to ideal Chern bands, where holomorphicity of the occupied states guarantees the vanishing of one chiral absorption rate with a quantized response for the other. In lattice realizations, such as the Harper-Hofstadter model, finite lattice-spacing corrections break the exact continuum metric description and give rise to a renormalized, emergent Landau-orbit metric; the probe ellipticity at which perfect dichroism is achieved then shifts accordingly, offering a direct spectroscopic window onto this lattice-induced geometric renormalization. Our results illuminate the rich geometric structure of quantum Hall phases and offer concrete pathways for observing these effects in quantum-engineered platforms.

</details>


### [158] [Interplay of Electrode Coupling Engineering, Quasiperiodicity, and Magnetic Flux in Quantum Transport through a Su-Schrieffer-Heeger Ring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28846)
*Sridhar,Souvik Roy,Malay Bandyopadhyay*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文揭示通过工程化电极-耦合构型可以根本性地重塑准周期量子系统中的相干输运现象。利用非平衡格林函数理论，系统分析了磁通贯穿的准周期Su-Schrieffer-Heeger环在对称和非对称多位点储层耦合下的电荷、热输运及电流涨落。研究发现多位点引线耦合将增强输运区域深入拓展到拓扑相，非对称源-漏耦合诱导出无序辅助导电相，磁通激活额外干涉通道的同时抑制了无序诱导的重入导电区，并驱动最优输运窗口从拓扑区向平庸跳跃区迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为准周期量子系统中的最优输运发生在均匀跳跃极限附近，本文旨在挑战这一认知，探索通过工程化电极-耦合构型来根本性地重塑输运图景的可能性，特别是在磁通调控与无序、拓扑及量子干涉的复杂相互作用下。

Method: 基于非平衡格林函数理论，对磁通贯穿的准周期Su-Schrieffer-Heeger环进行建模，系统考察了对称与非对称多位点储层耦合下的电荷输运、热输运及电流涨落特性。

Result: 1) 多位点引线耦合将增强输运区域拓展到拓扑相深处；2) 非对称源-漏耦合诱导出无序辅助导电相，准周期调制反而增强输运；3) 磁通激活额外干涉通道促进输运，同时抑制无序诱导的重入导电区；4) 磁通驱动最优输运窗口随无序强度增加从拓扑区向平庸跳跃区迁移。

Conclusion: 耦合工程可作为准周期材料中非平衡输运理性控制的有力范式，为实现通过无序、拓扑和量子干涉的相互作用精确调节输运特性的量子器件构型提供了路径。

Abstract: We reveal that engineering electrode-coupling configurations can fundamentally reshape coherent transport phenomena in quasiperiodic quantum systems. Leveraging nonequilibrium Green's function theory, we systematically analyze charge and heat transport, as well as current fluctuations, in a magnetic-flux-threaded quasiperiodic Su-Schrieffer-Heeger ring with both symmetric and asymmetric multi-site reservoir couplings. Contrary to the conventional expectation that optimal transport is achieved near the homogeneous-hopping limit, our results reveal that multi-site lead coupling fundamentally reshapes the transport landscape, extending the regime of enhanced transport deep into the topological phase. Strikingly, asymmetric source-drain coupling induces a disorder-assisted conducting phase where quasiperiodic modulation enhances, rather than suppresses, charge and energy transport. Magnetic flux exerts a dual influence: it activates additional interference-mediated transmission channels that amplify transport while simultaneously suppressing the disorder-induced re-entrant conducting regime. Furthermore, we uncover a flux-driven migration of the optimal transport window with increasing disorder strength, shifting from the topological regime toward the trivial-hopping regime. This behavior highlights the intricate interplay among quasiperiodicity, dimerization, magnetic-flux-induced quantum interference, and the geometry of the system-reservoir coupling. Collectively, our findings position coupling engineering as a powerful paradigm for the rational control of nonequilibrium transport in quasiperiodic materials and chart a route toward quantum device configurations in which transport characteristics can be precisely tuned via the interplay of disorder, topology, and quantum interference.

</details>


### [159] [Coherent and Incoherent Interfacial Spin Transport: Quantum-to-Classical Crossover in Spin Superfluids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28877)
*A. R. Moura,L. S. L. Barbosa*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过自洽谐波近似和微观线性响应理论，研究了NM/FMI/NM三层异质结构中界面自旋输运的热力学性质。发现界面自旋混合电导可分解为相干凝聚分量（由自旋超流体的宏观相驱动）和非相干涨落分量（由热致磁振子介导），并揭示了S=1/2极端量子极限下相干分量在有限温度消失、涨落分量呈现T²标度律及1/S抑制等关键行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立量子多体涨落与宏观自旋超流体水动力学之间的微观联系，为优化量子自旋电子器件中的长程相干输运提供基础原理。

Method: 采用自洽谐波近似（SCHA）结合微观线性响应理论，对由各向异性量子XXZ模型描述的NM/FMI/NM三层异质结构进行热力学分析，在全自旋区间评估界面自旋混合电导g↑↓。

Result: 发现g↑↓可唯一分解为：凝聚分量g_cond（由自旋超流体宏观相驱动）和涨落分量g_fluct（由随机热磁振子介导）。在S=1/2量子极限下，g_cond陡降并在有限相干温度T_coh处消失；g_fluct在T=0消失，低温下呈T²二次标度，随磁化强度增大受1/S抑制。

Conclusion: 研究成果桥接了量子多体涨落与宏观自旋超流体水动力学，为量子自旋电子器件中长程相干输运的优化提供了清晰的基础原理。

Abstract: We investigate the thermodynamics of interfacial spin transport within a normal metal/ferromagnetic insulator/normal metal ($\mathrm{NM/FMI/NM}$) trilayer heterostructure, where the central magnetic layer is described by the anisotropic quantum XXZ model. By employing the self-consistent harmonic approximation (SCHA) combined with a microscopic linear response formulation, we evaluate the interfacial spin-mixing conductance $g_{\uparrow\downarrow}$ across all spin regimes. We demonstrate that $g_{\uparrow\downarrow}$ uniquely decomposes into a coherent condensed component ($g_{\mathrm{cond}}$), driven by the macroscopic phase of the spin superfluid, and an incoherent fluctuation-driven term ($g_{\mathrm{fluct}}$) mediated by stochastic thermal magnons. Crucially, in the extreme quantum limit of $S = 1/2$, $g_{\mathrm{cond}}$ drops steeply and vanishes at a finite coherence temperature $T_{\mathrm{coh}}$. Conversely, the fluctuation-driven term $g_{\mathrm{fluct}}$ vanishes at $T = 0$, exhibits a characteristic $T^2$ quadratic scaling at low temperatures, and undergoes a systematic $1/S$ amplitude suppression as the macroscopic magnetization becomes robust. Our microscopic insights bridge the gap between quantum many-body fluctuations and macroscopic spin-superfluid hydrodynamics, providing clear foundational principles for optimizing long-range coherent transport in quantum spintronic devices.

</details>


### [160] [Reproducible Ohmic bismuth contacts to $\textrm{MoS}_2$ nanotubes and nanoribbons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28902)
*Robin T. K. Schock,Stefan Obloh,Korbinian Fink,Matthias Kronseder,Matjaž Malok,Maja Remškar,Andreas K. Hüttel*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文针对MoS₂纳米管和纳米线，展示了可重复制备金属接触的步骤，实现了无栅极下室温两点电阻中位值340 kΩ，并分析了不同制备方法的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoS₂纳米管和纳米带的接触电阻问题长期存在，由于弯曲小表面导致接触电阻分散，而平面MoS₂使用半金属已实现突破，因此需要为MoS₂纳米管/纳米线开发可重复的接触制备方法。

Method: 通过一系列工艺步骤优化，对气相生长的单个MoS₂纳米管和纳米线进行接触制备，并统计大量器件的室温两点电阻值，分析不同制备变更的影响。

Result: 所有器件在无栅极下均表现出有限的室温两点电阻，中位值为340 kΩ。

Conclusion: 实现了可重复的MoS₂纳米管/纳米线接触制备，解决了接触电阻分散问题，为场效应晶体管和量子电子器件应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: Attaching metallic contacts to transition metal dichalcogenide nanostructures and in particular to $\textrm{MoS}_2$ has posed significant challenges over the past years. For $\textrm{MoS}_2$ nanotubes and nanoribbons, a highly promising material for field effect transistors as well as quantum electronic devices, this is even more the case due to the small, curved surface. So far all attempts there have led to a wide scatter of contact resistances on the same chip. Recently, for quasi two-dimensional, flat $\textrm{MoS}_2$ flakes, the use of semimetals has led to a breakthrough, making transparent and Ohmic contacts possible. Here, we demonstrate the steps required to reproducibly fabricate contacts to single, vapor phase grown $\textrm{MoS}_2$ nanotubes and nanowires. All devices display finite room-temperature two-point resistances in absence of gating, with a median value of $340\,\textrm{k}Ω$ in a large fabrication series. A detailed analysis elucidates the impact of the different fabrication changes.

</details>


### [161] [Optically Switched Phonon Superradiance of Surface Acoustic Wave in Diamond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28935)
*Zhiwei Chen,Changyong Lei,Jie Ren*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出通过光驱动NV色心能级跃迁，在弱耦合条件下触发SAW声子超辐射相变，实现可调控的固态量子声子操控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管NV色心系综与SAW声子耦合可实现超辐射和集体量子控制，但实现可调谐的超辐射相变仍具挑战性。

Method: 通过光驱动NV色心率能级跃迁增强有效自旋-声子耦合，在弱耦合区域触发SAW声子超辐射相变。

Result: 超过临界阈值时，驱动光可快速开启声子超辐射，该动态效应在有限数量NV系综中依然存在。

Conclusion: 结果为固态量子器件中实现可调控的相干声子-NV自旋操控提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Surface acoustic wave (SAW) phonon coupling with nitrogen-vacancy (NV) center spins in diamond offers a promising platform for on-chip quantum phononic manipulations. Although an ensemble of NV centers coupled to a common SAW phonon mode enables superradiance and collective quantum control, achieving a tunable superradiant phase transition remains challenging. Here, we show that optically driving NV centers level transitions enhances the effective spin-phonon coupling, triggering a SAW phonon superradiant phase transition in the weak-coupling regime. We also demonstrate that above a critical threshold, the driving light rapidly switches on the phonon superradiance--a dynamic effect that persists in finite-number NV ensembles. Our results provide a controllable route to coherent phonon-NV spin manipulation in solid state quantum devices.

</details>


### [162] [Synchronic scattering and geometric dephasing in microwave-induced resistance oscillations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28977)
*Jesus Iñarrea*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的微波诱导电阻振荡（MIRO）量子输运模型，证明瞬时散射率直接受驱动相干态速度调制，并在ωt=2nπ时达到峰值，打破时间反演对称性产生净直流；同时引入退相位架构解释振幅饱和，通过非线性几何退相位(exp(-A/Rc))完美捕捉高强度下的线性到次线性功率交叉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MIRO理论无法完全解释高强场下振幅饱和及功率交叉行为，需要一种完全相干的非平衡输运描述来统一理解微波驱动下二维电子气的量子输运机制。

Method: 构建量子输运模型，证明驱动相干态速度直接调制瞬时散射率；引入非线性几何退相位机制(exp(-A/Rc))，描述振荡幅度A接近回旋半径Rc时的退相位效应。

Result: 散射率在ωt=2nπ时达到峰值（波包以最大速度扫过杂质势）；退相位机制精确捕捉了高功率下从线性到次线性的功率依赖关系交叉。

Conclusion: 该模型提供了MIRO现象的完全相干非平衡量子输运描述，统一解释了微波驱动下电阻振荡的强度饱和与功率交叉行为。

Abstract: We present a novel quantum transport model for microwave-induced resistance oscillations (MIRO) where we prove that the instantaneous scattering rate is directly modulated by the velocity of the driven coherent state. This interaction peaks exactly at $ωt = 2nπ$, where the wave packets sweep through the impurity landscape at maximum speed, breaking time-reversal symmetry to generate a net direct current. Additionally, we introduce a dephasing architecture to explain amplitude saturation: a non-linear geometric dephasing ($\exp(-A/R_c)$) triggered when the displacement amplitude $A$ of the oscillating coherent state,
  approaches the cyclotron radius $R_{c}$. This perfectly captures the linear-to-sublinear power crossover at high intensities, offering a fully coherent description of non-equilibrium transport.

</details>


### [163] [Electron Delocalization versus Emission Coherence of Quantum Dot Superlattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29262)
*Lanfang Hou,Zijian He,Kexin Wang,Kai Wang,Shun Wang,Butian Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过功率依赖的稳态光致发光和时间分辨光致发光衰减测量，研究了CdSe量子点超晶格中电子离域与协同辐射相干性之间的关系。结果表明，尽管量子共振峰表现出离域激子特征，但未观察到超线性强度标度或功率依赖的发射延迟等协同辐射特征。通过温度依赖光谱发现，不均匀展宽和低温暗激子参与导致的静态无序和激子态混合阻碍了宏观相干的建立。该工作提供了电子离域可与协同相干性解耦的直接实验证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理论研究中长期讨论电子离域与相干性之间的关系，特别是离域态是否必然产生协同辐射。本研究旨在通过实验直接回答这一未解决的问题。

Method: 采用功率依赖的稳态光致发光（PL）和时间分辨光致发光（TRPL）衰减测量，结合温度依赖光谱学分析方法。

Result: 量子共振峰具有离域激子特征，但未观察到超线性强度标度或功率依赖的发射延迟等协同辐射信号；温度依赖光谱揭示了不均匀展宽和低温暗激子参与。

Conclusion: 在CdSe量子点超晶格中，电子离域与协同辐射相干性可以解耦，无序效应（静态无序和激子态混合）是阻碍宏观相干建立的关键因素。

Abstract: Cooperative emission is a collective quantum optical process that requires macroscopic phase coherence among coupled emitters. Recent observations of cooperative emission in QD superlattices have renewed interest in how such coherence emerges in nanostructured solids. Meanwhile, theoretical studies have long discussed the relationship between electronic delocalization and coherence, particularly whether delocalized states necessarily give rise to cooperative emission. This study addresses this question through power-dependent steady-state PL and time-resolved PL decay measurements. The findings indicate that, although the quantum resonance peak exhibits delocalized excitonic characteristics, it shows no signatures of cooperative radiation. In particular, neither superlinear intensity scaling nor power-dependent emission delay was observed, indicating the absence of cooperative-radiation signatures. This can be understood from two disorder-related aspects. Temperature-dependent spectroscopy reveals pronounced inhomogeneous broadening and low-temperature dark-exciton participation, pointing to intra-domain static disorder and exciton-state mixing. These effects collectively hinder the establishment of macroscopic coherence. The temperature dependence of the quantum resonance peak decay lifetime is consistent with two-dimensional exciton dynamics. This work provides direct experimental evidence that electronic delocalization can be decoupled from cooperative coherence in CdSe quantum dot superlattices.

</details>


### [164] [Learning Inhomogeneous Heisenberg Hamiltonians in Nanographene Spin Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29281)
*Greta Lupi,Saketh Ravuri,Chenxiao Zhao,Weidan Zhang,Cesare Roncaglia,Renxiang Liu,Xinliang Feng,Daniele Passerone,Pascal Ruffieux,Roman Fasel,Jose L. Lado,Gonçalo Catarina*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于机器学习的局域方法，直接从非弹性扫描隧道谱图中重构空间调制的交换相互作用，在纳米石墨烯自旋链中成功识别了均匀与非均匀耦合区域，并定量再现了实验谱和激发隙的尺度行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低维自旋系统中交换相互作用常被假设为空间均匀，但结构和环境不均匀性会局域地改变耦合强度，现有方法无法有效捕捉这种空间调制。

Method: 采用与长度无关的局域机器学习方法，从非弹性扫描隧道谱图直接重构空间调制的交换相互作用。

Result: 在纳米石墨烯自旋链中识别出近均匀和非均匀耦合区域；重构模型定量复现实验谱；正确恢复激发隙随系统尺寸的标度关系。

Conclusion: 建立了一种将局域谱学测量与有效多体哈密顿量联系起来的通用策略。

Abstract: Inferring microscopic Hamiltonians from experimental data is a central challenge in quantum materials and quantum simulation. In low-dimensional spin systems, exchange interactions are often assumed to be spatially uniform, despite structural and environmental inhomogeneities that can locally modify the coupling. Here, we leverage a local, length-independent machine learning methodology to reconstruct spatially modulated exchange interactions directly from inelastic scanning tunneling spectroscopy maps. We demonstrate this approach with nanographene spin chains, identifying both near-uniform and inhomogeneous regimes across the synthesized magnets. The reconstructed models quantitatively reproduce the experimental spectra and recover the correct scaling of the excitation gap with system size. Our results establish a general strategy to bridge local spectroscopic measurements with effective many-body Hamiltonians.

</details>


### [165] [Emergent energy scales in magnonic systems with relative motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29316)
*Daigo Oue*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文以磁振子系统为例，系统阐述了相对运动通过多普勒效应产生涌现能量尺度，进而驱动非平衡输运与参量不稳定性等非平衡现象的机制，并建立了与量子摩擦、切伦科夫辐射和Zeldovich超辐射等现象的统一理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示相对运动系统中涌现能量尺度的物理起源，为理解无传统驱动力（如温度梯度、化学势差）条件下非平衡输运和动力学不稳定性提供统一机制。

Method: 以磁振子系统为具体平台，从微扰（运动诱导磁振子输运）和非微扰（运动诱导参量不稳定性、磁振子对自发产生）两个区域逐步展开分析，并类比量子摩擦、切伦科夫辐射和Zeldovich超辐射等已知现象。

Result: 阐明了相对运动引入的多普勒频率尺度作为有效非平衡偏压，驱动磁振子流；当相对速度超过临界阈值时，涌现能量尺度可共振产生磁振子对，导致微扰描述失效、真空失稳，从而大幅增强输运和非平衡动力学。

Conclusion: 涌现能量尺度为理解相对运动量子系统中的输运现象和动力学不稳定性提供了统一的理论框架。

Abstract: Relative motion between interacting systems can generate emergent energy scales that are absent in isolated systems. While uniform motion can be eliminated by a Galilean transformation, relative motion between interacting systems generally cannot. In the presence of characteristic spatial structures, relative motion gives rise to a Doppler frequency scale determined by the characteristic wavevector of the excitation and the relative velocity of the system. This emergent scale provides a fundamental mechanism for driving nonequilibrium phenomena in moving systems. In particular, the emergent energy scale is determined by how the relative motion probes the spatial structure of the relevant excitation. In this tutorial, we illustrate these ideas using magnonic systems as a concrete platform. We first discuss motion-induced magnon transport between relatively moving ferromagnets, in which the Doppler frequency serves as an effective nonequilibrium bias in the perturbative regime. This mechanism produces magnon currents even in the absence of conventional driving forces such as temperature gradients or chemical potential differences. We then introduce motion-induced parametric instabilities. When the emergent scale becomes sufficiently large to resonantly create magnon pairs, the perturbative description breaks down, and the magnonic vacuum becomes unstable. Above a critical velocity threshold, spontaneous magnon-pair creation emerges, resulting in strongly enhanced transport and nonequilibrium dynamics. Connections to related phenomena, including quantum friction, Cherenkov emission, and Zeldovich superradiance, are also highlighted. The concept of an emergent energy scale provides a unifying framework for understanding transport phenomena and instabilities in quantum systems with relative motion.

</details>


### [166] [Quantum Theory of Current-Generating Local Orbital Magnetization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29330)
*Akito Daido*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文推导了非相互作用电子局域轨道磁化的量子力学公式，通过大势的局域通量响应来定义，并在二维和三维系统中分别验证了其唯一性与合理性，最后用Haldane模型进行了示例说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的局域轨道磁化公式未能同时满足与平衡电流密度的旋度关系以及现代体轨道磁化理论，因此需要推导一个自洽的量子力学局域公式。

Method: 通过考虑大势对局域通量的响应，推导出局域轨道磁化的量子力学公式；在二维中公式唯一确定，在三维中通过纵向模糊性选择自然代表；进一步通过粗粒化得到局域标记量，并验证其产生电流到三阶导数项，且其位点位置矩等于有限尺寸系统的轨道磁四极矩。

Result: 得到了一个自洽的局域轨道磁化量子力学公式，在二维中唯一，在三维中具有自然代表性；粗粒化后的局域标记可产生电流至三阶导数项；位点位置矩与轨道磁四极矩一致；Haldane模型验证了结果。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了局域轨道磁化公式的缺失，提供了与电流关系和现代体理论相一致的自洽框架，为相关材料的第一性原理计算奠定了基础。

Abstract: Local orbital magnetization is the field whose rotation generates the equilibrium current density. Unlike spin magnetization, a quantum-mechanical local formula consistent with both this current relation and the modern theory of bulk orbital magnetization has been missing. In this work, we derive a quantum-mechanical formula for the local orbital magnetization for non-interacting electrons by considering local-flux response of the grand potential. The local-flux response fixes the formula uniquely in two dimensions, whereas in three dimensions it selects a natural representative within a longitudinal ambiguity. Furthermore, coarse graining yields a natural local marker that generates the current to third-derivative order, and its site-position moment equals the orbital magnetic quadrupole moment of finite-size systems. We illustrate the obtained results with the Haldane model.

</details>


### [167] [Probing Quantum Geometric Phases via Scanning Tunneling Microscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29564)
*Chao Yan,Mu-Wei Gao,Yue Zhao,Jia-Xin Yin*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文综述了利用扫描隧道显微镜/谱学（STM/STS）在实空间直接可视化量子几何相位的四种代表性方法：纳米尺度实空间干涉测量Aharonov-Bohm相位、缺陷诱导的准粒子干涉提取Berry相位、对称系统中的序参量分解（如魔角石墨烯）以及数值2D锁相技术映射非常规超导体中配对密度波和电荷密度波的相位纹理与拓扑缺陷，建立了量子相位到实空间可观测量的翻译框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子几何相位是电子波函数几何、拓扑及多体关联的核心决定因素，但传统方法依赖动量空间探针或宏观输运测量，受限于体技术的空间平均效应，无法在实空间直接可视化和量化相位结构。

Method: 综述了四种代表性方法：(1) 纳米尺度实空间干涉测量AB几何相位；(2) 缺陷诱导的准粒子干涉与波前位错提取Berry相位；(3) 魔角石墨烯等对称系统中序参量分解重构复相位结构；(4) 数值2D锁相技术映射非常规超导体中PDW和CDW的相位纹理与拓扑缺陷。

Result: 实现了量子相位向实空间和局域可观测量的直接翻译，使相分辨STM成像能够对拓扑物态、对称性破缺模式和强电子关联提供严格约束。

Conclusion: 建立了实空间相位工程的鲁棒框架，为量子材料中原位相位调控奠定了基础。

Abstract: The quantum geometric phase intrinsically dictates the geometry, topology, and many-body correlations of electronic wave functions. While quantum geometric phases are conventionally inferred through momentum-space probes or macroscopic transport measurements, their direct visualization and quantification in real space have historically been restricted by the spatial averaging of bulk techniques. Scanning tunneling microscopy and spectroscopy (STM/STS) circumvent this limitation, leveraging atomic-scale spatial resolution and high energy sensitivity to resolve local electronic phase profiles directly. This review highlights recent progress across four representative methodologies: probing the Aharonov-Bohm (AB) geometric phase via nanoscale real space interferometry; extracting the Berry phase from defect-induced quasiparticle interference and wavefront dislocations; reconstructing the complex phase structure in symmetric systems, such as magic-angle graphene, using order parameter decomposition; and mapping the phase textures and topological defects of pair density wave (PDW) and charge density wave (CDW) in unconventional superconductors utilizing the numerical 2D lock-in technique. Together, these developments show how quantum phases can be translated onto real space and locally resolvable observables. Phase-resolved STM imaging provides stringent constraints on topological states of matter, symmetry-breaking patterns, and strong electronic correlations, outlining a robust framework for in situ phase engineering in quantum materials.

</details>


### [168] [Length--Velocity Gauge Equivalence of Quantum Geometric Nonlinear Conductivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29663)
*Shakeel Ahmad,Fei Xue*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过发展规范一致的密度矩阵理论，解决了非线性二阶直流响应中不同规范（长度规范和速度规范）看似给出不同静态极限的歧义，证明了两种规范在正确处理下等价，且净余响应归结为费米面上的量子几何贡献，完全带隙绝缘体在绝热干净极限下无残余非线性霍尔电流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性输运已成为探测超越贝里曲率物理的量子几何的灵敏探针，但内禀二阶直流响应在概念上存在微妙之处：不同的量子与半经典公式在静态极限下看似给出不同结果，且费米海与费米面贡献的归属存在分歧。本文旨在解决这一歧义。

Method: 发展了一种规范一致的密度矩阵理论，分别在长度规范（电场通过位置算符耦合）和速度规范（电场通过矢势进入）下推导内禀非线性电导率，并确保两种规范采用相同的推迟延拓方式以及速度规范电流包含所有场依赖顶点。

Result: 证明了两种规范在正确条件下给出相同的绝热直流响应；表观费米海项在完整表达式中相互抵消，仅留下由能带归一化量子度量决定的费米面量子几何贡献；完全带隙绝缘体在绝热干净极限下无残余直流非线性霍尔电流；费米面项的反应部分与原始半经典贝里连接极化率响应一致，耗散欧姆部分则需要更谨慎地处理弛豫和杂质散射。

Conclusion: 建立了长度-速度规范等价性对于量子几何非线性响应的有效性，为利用非线性输运探测磁量子几何（特别是在PT对称反铁磁体中）提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Nonlinear transport has emerged as a sensitive probe of quantum geometry beyond the Berry-curvature physics of linear response. However, the intrinsic second-order dc response remains conceptually subtle: different quantum and semiclassical formulations can appear to give different static limits, with different assignments of Fermi sea and Fermi surface contributions. Here we resolve this ambiguity by developing a gauge-consistent density-matrix theory of intrinsic nonlinear conductivity in both the length gauge, where the electric field couples through the position operator, and the velocity gauge, where it enters through the vector potential. We show that the two gauges give the same adiabatic dc response when the same retarded continuation is used for all external frequencies and when the velocity gauge current includes all field-dependent vertices. The apparent Fermi sea terms cancel in the full expression, leaving a Fermi surface quantum geometric contribution determined by the band-normalized quantum metric. This result implies that a fully gapped insulator has no residual dc nonlinear Hall current in the adiabatic clean limit. The reactive part of the Fermi surface term agrees with the original semiclassical Berry-connection-polarizability response, while the dissipative Ohmic sector requires a more careful treatment of relaxation and impurity scattering. Our work establishes the length-velocity gauge equivalence for quantum geometric nonlinear response and provides a foundation for using nonlinear transport to probe magnetic quantum geometry, especially in PT-symmetric antiferromagnets.

</details>


### [169] [Spin-1 Dirac dispersion and Chern insulating phases in 2D honeycomb Sierpiński fractal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29827)
*Shneha Biswas,Shouya Yoshida,Katsunori Wakabayashi,Sudipta Dutta*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 基于石墨烯的Sierpiński分形结构在最近邻紧束缚模型中存在零能手性模和自旋-1狄拉克色散。考虑Haldane哈密顿量中的次近邻跳跃和Semenoff质量项后，原本的平带变得有色散，并引入丰富拓扑相，最大陈数可达±3，超越了传统蜂窝晶格。这为通过外场实现二维周期分形中的拓扑相变提供了可能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索石墨烯基Sierpiński分形中超出传统蜂窝晶格的拓扑相，以及时间反演和空间反演对称性破缺对能带结构的影响。

Method: 在最近邻紧束缚模型基础上，引入Haldane哈密顿量中的次近邻跳跃（局域通量）和Semenoff交错质量项，分别破坏时间反演和空间反演对称性，通过陈数表征拓扑相。

Result: 对称性破缺使平带变得有色散，并引入陈数高达±3的丰富拓扑相，超越了传统蜂窝晶格的拓扑特性。

Conclusion: 这为在石墨烯之外的二维周期分形中通过外场调控实现拓扑相变开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Graphene-based Sierpiński fractals host a zero-energy chiral mode and spin-1 Dirac dispersions within the nearest-neighbor tight-binding model. However, the presence of complex next-nearest neighbor hopping arising from the local flux and the staggered Semenoff mass terms, modeled within the Haldane Hamiltonian, breaks the time-reversal and spatial inversion symmetries, respectively, and makes these flat bands dispersive. Moreover, they introduce rich topological phases in this class of systems that can be characterized by Chern numbers up to $\pm 3$, i.e., beyond the conventional honeycomb lattice. These observations pave the way for the exploration of 2D periodic fractals beyond graphene, where topological phase transitions can be realized through externally applied fields.

</details>


### [170] [Anomalous Duffing mechanics of a suspended carbon nanotube quantum dot at ultrastrong coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30099)
*Akong N. Loh,Furkan R. Özyiğit,Fabian Stadler,Katrin Burkert,Niklas Hüttner,Andreas K. Hüttel*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在低温环境下，悬浮单壁碳纳米管量子点同时作为原型量子点和高品质机械谐振器。单电子隧穿实现了超强电子-振动耦合，高品质因子下呈现强非线性Duffing响应。研究发现单电子隧穿区域边缘Duffing参数局部增大（硬化弹簧效应），与理论预期的软化弹簧行为相反，讨论了可能的原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定量研究悬浮单壁碳纳米管量子点中Duffing响应参数及其与库仑阻塞振荡的关系，以理解电子-振动耦合机制下的非线性力学行为。

Method: 在低温条件下测量悬浮单壁碳纳米管量子点的Duffing响应参数，分析其与单电子隧穿区域（库仑阻塞振荡）的对应关系，定量观测Duffing参数的空间分布。

Result: 在单电子隧穿区域边缘观察到Duffing参数局部增大，表现为硬化弹簧效应，其大小和近似标度行为与单电子隧穿现象一致。

Conclusion: 观测到的硬化弹簧效应与理论预期的单电子隧穿导致的软化弹簧行为相反，这一矛盾结果的原因尚需进一步探讨。

Abstract: At cryogenic temperatures, suspended single-wall carbon nanotube quantum dots act both as prototypical quantum dots as well as high-quality factor mechanical resonators. Single-electron tunneling enables reaching an ultrastrong electron-vibron coupling regime, where the coupling parameter exceeds the vibration frequency. Due to the high quality factors, a strongly nonlinear Duffing response is easily reached. Here, we quantitatively study the Duffing response parameters of such a device and their relation to Coulomb blockade oscillation. At the edges of single-electron tunneling regions, a local increase of the Duffing parameter corresponding to a stiffening spring is observed. Size and approximate scaling of the effect agree with single-electron tunneling phenomena, which however should lead to softening spring behaviour. Possible causes of these puzzling results are discussed.

</details>


### [171] [Rolling Two-Dimensional Collinear Magnets into Chiral Nanotubes with $p$-Wave Magnetism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30214)
*Zhejunyu Jin,Robin R. Neumann,Rodrigo Jaeschke-Ubiergo,Jairo Sinova,Alexander Mook*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文证明，将二维共线磁体（铁磁体、反铁磁体和交变磁体）卷曲成纳米管，可以产生丰富的自旋对称性景观，其中手性纳米管中具有径向或切向共面自旋纹理的结构能够实现p波磁性，且非相对论性Edelstein效应比传统自旋轨道驱动的电荷-自旋转换效率高一个数量级以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究p波磁体能否从简单且广泛可得的共线磁性构建块中产生，而非必须依赖于 intrinsically 非共线磁序。

Method: 将二维共线磁体（铁磁体、反铁磁体、交变磁体）卷曲成纳米管，通过曲率、手性和磁序调控自旋对称性，并结合电子和磁振子谱分析。

Result: 手性纳米管中径向或切向共面自旋纹理可实现p波磁性，在电子和磁振子谱中均表现为反对称p波自旋劈裂；非相对论性Edelstein响应超过传统自旋轨道驱动转换一个数量级以上。

Conclusion: 磁性纳米管为工程化非常规p波磁性提供了多功能平台，并预测了远超传统机制的电荷-自旋转换效率。

Abstract: $p$-wave magnets are noncollinear compensated magnetic systems that exhibit nonrelativistic antisymmetric spin splitting in momentum space. Their odd-parity spin symmetry enables unconventional spintronic functionalities, including highly efficient charge-to-spin conversion via the Edelstein effect. An outstanding question is whether such magnetic phases can emerge from simple and broadly accessible magnetic building blocks rather than from intrinsically noncollinear magnetic orders. Here, we show that rolling two-dimensional collinear magnets -- ferromagnets, antiferromagnets, and altermagnets -- into nanotubes generates a rich spin-symmetry landscape controlled by curvature, chirality, and magnetic order. Remarkably, chiral nanotubes hosting radial or tangential coplanar spin textures generically realize $p$-wave magnetism irrespective of the underlying collinear parent phase. The emergent odd-parity spin symmetry manifests itself in both electronic and magnonic spectra through antisymmetric $p$-wave spin splitting. Our results establish magnetic nanotubes as a versatile platform for engineering unconventional $p$-wave magnetism and predict a nonrelativistic Edelstein response that exceeds conventional spin-orbit-driven charge-to-spin conversion by more than an order of magnitude.

</details>


### [172] [Transport in extended Kitaev chain with time reversal symmetry breaking and long-range interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30483)
*Averi Banerjee,Syeda Rafisa Rahaman,Nilanjan Bondyopadhaya*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个由长程相互作用的Kitaev链与金属引线构成的混合结，探讨了时间反演对称性破缺对电输运特性的影响，发现长程与短程Kitaev链在TRS破缺下的输运行为存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究时间反演对称性破缺与相互作用范围（长程vs短程）对Kitaev链电输运特性的耦合影响，揭示两者 interplay 导致的物理新现象。

Method: 构建由扩展一维Kitaev链（含TRS破缺和长程相互作用）夹在两侧金属引线之间的混合结，在电压偏置下研究电输运，通过改变TRS破缺强度，对比长程与短程Kitaev链的输运特性。

Result: TRS破缺会改变态密度和本征态的局域/退局域性质，进而影响输运特性；长程与短程Kitaev链对TRS破缺的响应并不相同，两者输运特性存在明显差异。

Conclusion: 时间反演对称性破缺与相互作用范围的协同效应会导致可观测的输运性质差异，这为区分长程和短程Kitaev链提供了物理依据。

Abstract: We consider a junction consisting of an extended one-dimensional Kitaev chain which incorporates both time-reversal symmetry (TRS) breaking and long-range interaction, sandwiched between two metallic leads from two sides. In this hybrid device, we study electrical transport under voltage bias for varying strength of the TRS breaking phase. We compare the transport characteristics of long-range type Kitaev chain with that of the short-range Kitaev chain as the strength of the TRS breaking phase varies. We find that the TRS breaking modifies the density of states and localisation/delocalisation property of the eigenstates which in turn affect the transport characteristics. Moreover, we find that the impact of the TRS breaking is not identical for the long-range Kitaev chain and its short-range counterpart. Therefore, noticeable differences in the transport properties can be observed due to the interplay between the TRS breaking and the range of interaction.

</details>


### [173] [Topological control of third-harmonic generation in a mesoscopic quantum ring with spiral dislocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30245)
*Carlos Magno O. Pereira,Denise Assafrão,Edilberto O. Silva*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究具有螺旋位错的二维介质量子环的非线性光学响应，重点分析三次谐波产生（THG）。螺旋位错通过扭转诱导的空间变形建模，改变有效度规但不引入曲率。结合弯曲空间中的最小耦合、径向环约束和垂直磁场，推导有效径向薛定谔问题，获得束缚态，并在电偶极近似下评估非线性极化率。研究表明，拓扑变形环的轴对称性保留了偶极选择定则Δm=±1，抑制了二次谐波产生，但THG通过多步跃迁链仍被允许。三种互补分析进一步扩展了研究：退相干控制的谱分辨率、三维瀑布谱（依赖β和B）、以及THG振幅的通道分辨分解。这些结果确立了螺旋位错作为调控介观环状纳米结构非线性光学活性的鲁棒几何旋钮。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索拓扑缺陷（螺旋位错）如何影响介观量子环的非线性光学响应，特别是三次谐波产生，以揭示几何变形作为调控光学性质的新自由度。

Method: 采用弯曲空间中的最小耦合方案，结合螺旋位错导致的扭转变形度规、径向环约束和垂直磁场，求解有效径向薛定谔方程得到束缚态，再在电偶极近似下计算非线性极化率。通过退相干控制光谱、三维瀑布谱和通道分辨分解三种分析方法拓展研究。

Result: 螺旋位错不破坏轴对称性，偶极选择定则Δm=±1保持有效，二次谐波产生被抑制，而三次谐波产生通过多步跃迁链仍然允许。THG谱对位错参数β和磁场B敏感可调。

Conclusion: 螺旋位错作为一种鲁棒的几何调控手段，可有效调节介观环状纳米结构中的非线性光学活性，特别在THG方面展现出可调谐潜力。

Abstract: We investigate the nonlinear optical response of a two-dimensional mesoscopic quantum ring subjected to a spiral dislocation, with emphasis on third-harmonic generation (THG). The topological defect is modeled through a torsion-induced deformation of space, which modifies the effective metric without introducing curvature. By combining the minimal-coupling prescription in curved space with a radial ring confinement and a perpendicular magnetic field, we derive the effective radial Schrödinger problem, obtain the bound states, and evaluate the nonlinear susceptibilities within the electric-dipole approximation. We show that the axial symmetry of the topologically deformed ring preserves the dipole selection rule $Δm=\pm 1$ and therefore suppresses second-harmonic generation, while THG remains allowed through multistep transition chains. The study is further expanded through three complementary analyses that can be implemented without changing the Hamiltonian: a dephasing-controlled study of spectral resolution, three-dimensional waterfall spectra showing the dependence on $β$ and $B$, and a channel-resolved decomposition of the THG amplitude. Together, these results establish the spiral dislocation as a robust geometric knob for tuning nonlinear optical activity in mesoscopic ring-shaped nanostructures.

</details>


### [174] [Heat rectification through a quantum two-level system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30465)
*Tsuyoshi Yamamoto,Manuel Houzet*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过张量网络方法研究量子二能级系统与两个热库非对称耦合的热整流效应，发现了从紫外到红外标度区的流动行为，并基于红外固定点附近的微扰理论推导出整流比普适幂律，数值结果与理论预测吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究耗散诱导的多体物理效应对热输运的影响，理解量子二能级系统在强关联区间的热整流机制，特别是标度行为背后的普适规律。

Method: 采用张量网络方法计算欧姆型自旋-玻色子模型的稳态热流，在弱耦合和非相干隧穿等极限区间与解析公式交叉验证，并在红外固定点附近应用微扰理论分析整流比标度律。

Result: 发现了从紫外区（温度高于近藤温度）到红外区（温度低于近藤温度）的标度流动行为，整流比在红外区遵循普适幂律，数值结果与解析预测一致。

Conclusion: 揭示了耗散诱导的多体物理对热输运的影响机制，为理解量子热整流提供了基础性认识。

Abstract: We study heat rectification through a quantum two-level system asymmetrically coupled to two thermal baths, as described by the Ohmic spin-boson model. We evaluate the steady-state heat current using a tensor-network approach, which enables us to access the strongly correlated regime, and benchmark the results against analytical formulas in several limiting regimes, including the weak-coupling and incoherent-tunneling regimes. We identify a scaling regime where the studied system flows from an ultraviolet regime, at temperatures larger than the Kondo temperature, to an infrared regime, at temperatures lower than the Kondo temperature. By applying perturbation theory near the infrared fixed point, we find that the rectification ratio follows a universal power law. Our numerical results agree well with this analytical prediction. Our results provide a fundamental understanding of how dissipation-induced many-body physics affects heat transport.

</details>


### [175] [GaAs/AlAs Acoustic Nanocavities for Coherent GHz-THz Phonon Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30510)
*S. Sandeep,E. R. Cardozo de Oliveira,E. Mehdi,N. D. Lanzillotti-Kimura*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文综述了基于GaAs/AlAs声学纳米腔作为GHz-THz声子工程平台的研究进展，重点介绍了分布式布拉格反射镜（DBR）架构和微柱谐振器实现的三维声子限域与光声场共局域化，以及超快光学技术在纳米尺度声子动力学、相干性和耗散研究中的应用，并展望了其在非线性声子学、混合量子系统和集成声子电路中的前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高频率声学声子在半导体纳米结构中的受控限域对于功能纳米声子学和混合量子技术至关重要，需要一种兼具可扩展性和优异性能的通用平台。

Method: 采用GaAs/AlAs异质结构，基于分布式布拉格反射镜（DBR）架构和微柱谐振器实现三维声子限域与光声场共局域化，结合超快光学技术（皮秒超声学和布里渊散射）进行声子动力学研究。

Result: GaAs/AlAs平台展示了成熟的 epitaxial 生长、强光弹耦合、GHz-THz波段光声模式共局域化等优势，实现了受限声学模式的高效相干产生、检测和操控，但在声光转换效率和可扩展电控方面仍面临挑战。

Conclusion: GaAs/AlAs异质结构可作为下一代纳米声子功能器件的稳健可扩展平台，在非线性声子学、混合量子系统和集成声子电路领域具有重要前景。

Abstract: The controlled confinement of high-frequency acoustic phonons in semiconductor nanostructures has emerged as a key ingredient for functional nanophononic and hybrid quantum technologies. In this Review, we summarize recent advances that have established GaAs/AlAs acoustic nanocavities as a versatile and scalable platform for GHz-THz phonon engineering. Compared with alternative nanophononic platforms, GaAs/AlAs offers a particularly favorable combination of mature epitaxial growth, strong photoelastic coupling, and simultaneous optical-acoustic mode colocalization across the GHz-THz regime. We focus on distributed Bragg reflector (DBR)-based architectures, with particular emphasis on micropillar resonators enabling three-dimensional phonon confinement and strong colocalization of acoustic and optical fields. Recent developments in ultrafast optical techniques, including picosecond ultrasonics and Brillouin scattering, have provided unprecedented access to phonon dynamics, coherence, and dissipation at the nanoscale. These advances, combined with strong optophononic coupling, have enabled efficient coherent generation, detection, and manipulation of confined acoustic modes. We discuss key performance metrics, integration strategies, and remaining challenges, notably in acousto-optic transduction efficiency and scalable electrical control. Finally, we outline near-term perspectives for nonlinear phononics, hybrid quantum systems, and integrated phononic circuits, positioning GaAs/AlAs heterostructures as a robust and scalable platform for next-generation nanophononic functionalities.

</details>


### [176] [Microfabricated Au and Au/graphene bilayer platelets for levitation experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30591)
*Sunghyun Kim,Joyce E. Coppock,B. E. Kane*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种制备微米级Au和Au/石墨烯双层片状悬浮液的工艺，采用薄膜沉积、光刻、离子铣削、HF刻蚀及离心纯化等方法，获得高质量、低聚集的单分散片状悬浮液，产率高达30%，适用于悬浮纳米粒子实验等领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以制备高质量、低聚集的微米级金属和金属/二维材料双层片状悬浮液，限制了悬浮纳米粒子实验等应用的发展。

Method: 采用薄膜沉积、光学光刻、离子铣削、HF刻蚀基底、离心纯化去除残留HF，最终通过电喷雾沉积进行表征。

Result: 制备的片状颗粒聚集极少，整体产率可达原始图案化片状颗粒的30%；Au/石墨烯双层结构在制备、释放和电喷雾沉积过程中保持完整。

Conclusion: 该工艺为制备高质量片状悬浮液提供了一条实用路径，适用于悬浮纳米粒子实验及其他需要二维纳米结构悬浮液的应用。

Abstract: We describe a fabrication process for preparing liquid suspensions of micron-scale Au and Au/graphene bilayer platelets using thin-film deposition, optical lithography, ion milling, hydrofluoric acid (HF) substrate etching, and release from the substrate into a liquid suspension. Residual HF is removed through repeated centrifugation, decanting, and dilution cycles. The resulting suspension is characterized by electrospray deposition onto a secondary substrate, followed by electron and atomic force microscopy. The deposited platelets exhibit minimal aggregation, and the overall platelet yield reaches up to 30% of the platelets originally patterned on the wafer. Lateral force microscopy further confirms that the Au/graphene bilayer remains intact throughout fabrication, release, and electrospray deposition. This process provides a practical route for preparing high-quality platelet suspensions for levitated nanoparticle experiments and other applications requiring suspensions of two-dimensional nanostructures.

</details>
